# Disk E: audit — 2026-11-16

**Read-only inventory. No pre-existing item was deleted, moved or modified. No tests, builds, pre-ff, cleanup commands or server restart were run. Source: `9a48765744f32f467f6e53c1f85e94921cbdd54b`. No Git identity/email settings, chat messages or personal-database contents are published; email-looking path fragments and local user SIDs are redacted.**

Snapshot: 2026-11-15T23:23:34+00:00 to 2026-11-15T23:24:31+00:00 (UTC), 57.13 seconds. Report date uses Europe/Moscow (2026-11-16).

## Capacity and measurement method

- Total: **465.761 GiB** (500,106,784,768 bytes).
- Used: **451.843 GiB**; free: **13.917 GiB** at scan end.
- One recursive volume traversal: 708,193 files, 76,193 directories; no second volume size scan.
- 107 reparse directories were not followed; cloud placeholders were not hydrated. `E:/iCloudDrive` is therefore **not measured**, not an empty folder. `E:/System Volume Information` was inaccessible and is **not measured**.
- Tables use per-path **logical sizes** and folder-entry last-write timestamps in UTC. Parent/child rows overlap and must not be summed. Allocation metadata was also queried without reading file contents; there were 0 allocation-query errors.
- Observed hardlinks duplicate approximately 8.689 GiB in per-path allocation totals; aliases/junction targets were not multiplied. The safe reclaim estimate accounts for single-link allocations and includes hardlinks only when all observed links are inside the safe set. NTFS metadata, excluded/system/cloud content and live activity explain differences from volume used space.
- The volume size scanner reads metadata and small QA ownership markers only. Targeted follow-up read-only Git status/diff checks compare candidate worktrees with existing archived patches to avoid losing unique changes; this is not a second recursive volume-size traversal. Git ran with optional locks disabled. No branch/index/config mutation was performed during the audit. Audit output created after the snapshot is not included in snapshot sizes.

## Short findings

- The largest individual file is `E:/stl.zip`: **139.960 GiB**, class **UNCLEAR**. A filename/age is not proof that it can be discarded.
- `qa-tmp` contains large replay clones, but modified trial clones and unique commits must not be treated as junk by name.
- All `qa-evidence`, historical `qa-artifacts`, review galleries (including sweep-polish GALLERY.html), archives, the running preview package and TLS material are KEEP until release/explicit retirement.
- **SAFE_TO_DELETE: 36 disjoint targets; 41.139 GiB logical / 41.269 GiB allocated by path; estimated reclaimable 41.269 GiB.** These are proposals only; commands below were NOT executed.

### Stronger checks used for safe clones

- Archived official-update clones: current HEAD and tracked binary diff match the previously archived, SHA256-verified gzip patch; no untracked files. Only the 14 clones whose commits also remain reachable through main-repository refs are safe. Four modified trial clone commits are not held by those refs and remain UNCLEAR despite preserved patches.
- Other scratch clones: only standalone clones with clean tracked/untracked status and HEAD held by main-repository refs are safe (13 of 28 checked). Modified trial directories are not swept with their parent.
- Project marker containers: keep=true is always preserved; keep=false requires a dead recorded PID, no retained child, no Git repository and a disposable work/browser role. QA result-output containers are retained rather than inferred disposable from size.

## Owner commands — NOT EXECUTED

**Do not enable application until the owner separately approves these exact paths.** The script defaults to WhatIf, verifies fully qualified bounded target paths and rejects reparse roots/live processes. Use PowerShell 7+ so child junctions are removed as links rather than traversed. It never targets qa-evidence, registered external worktrees, the current checkout, Public firewall settings, release/Pages, or the preview tree. No force Git operation is involved.

$allowed = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath('E:/Warhammer/qa-tmp/')
$checked = @()
$processes = @(Get-CimInstance Win32_Process | Where-Object ProcessId -ne $PID)
foreach ($target in $targets) {
    $item = Get-Item -LiteralPath $target -ErrorAction Stop
    $full = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($item.FullName)
    if (-not $full.StartsWith($allowed, [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw 'Out-of-scope target' }
    $preview = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath('E:/Warhammer/qa-tmp/preview-v1')
    if ($full -ieq $preview -or $full.StartsWith($preview + [IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar, [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw 'Preview is protected' }
    if (($item.Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0) { throw 'Reparse root refused' }
    $needle = $full.Replace([char]47, [char]92)
    if ($processes | Where-Object { $_.CommandLine -and $_.CommandLine.Replace([char]47, [char]92).IndexOf($needle, [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase) -ge 0 }) { throw ('Live process refers to ' + $full) }
    $checked += $full
}
foreach ($full in $checked) {
    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $full -Recurse -Force -WhatIf:(-not $Apply)
}
